# DynamoDB-compatible storage benchmarks

Preload: 25 million 800-byte items, three ALL GSIs: 20 GB of main-table data and approximately 80 GB of total logical data.

## Stop points and comparisons

Splits are **read/write**. Each ramp stopped when pooled read p90 exceeded 50 ms, pooled write p90 exceeded 80 ms, or unexpected errors exceeded 2%. Rates are API requests/s, not items/s.

Each row is one backend's terminal stage. Cells show **offered → achieved req/s; read / write p90 ms; unexpected errors %**. Bold cells identify the backend stopping. At ExtendDB's early stops, other backends use their closest measured offered rate, shown explicitly. “—” means the other backend's ramp had already ended. “Unavailable” means pooled write p90 is absent from the retained report at that rate. No values are extrapolated. AuxStorage/PostgreSQL stopped on admission queue-timeout 503s in all splits. FoundationDB stopped on read p90 in all splits; its 90/10 terminal stage also exceeded the error cutoff. AuxStorage/SQLite stopped on write p90 in all splits, with read p90 also over the cutoff in 10/90.

### Last passing stages

| Backend                 | 90/10 offered → achieved req/s | 50/50 offered → achieved req/s | 10/90 offered → achieved req/s |
| ----------------------- | -----------------------------: | -----------------------------: | -----------------------------: |
| AuxStorage/PostgreSQL   |                 2610 → 2585.37 |                 2146 → 2099.92 |                 1842 → 1804.47 |
| AuxStorage/FoundationDB |                 2562 → 2552.56 |                 1394 → 1356.11 |                   818 → 814.61 |
| AuxStorage/SQLite       |                 1154 → 1152.26 |                   258 → 255.63 |                   146 → 145.83 |
| Dynoxide                |                   834 → 833.14 |                   370 → 368.78 |                   242 → 241.21 |
| ExtendDB                |                     37 → 36.99 |                   261 → 258.08 |                           None |

ExtendDB started at 5 req/s after the original 50 req/s 90/10 attempt immediately exceeded the write cutoff. Its 10/90 result contains only 25 requests and one query returning 21 items from gsi1; it establishes neither a passing rate nor measured coverage of all query sources.

## Capacity and 50/50 latency charts

RCU/WCU are calculated logical capacity equivalents from validated successful work, including index work; they are not native billing or physical I/O. Capacity timelines concatenate 90/10, 50/50 and 10/90 windows, omitting rests and runner replacement gaps. Latency bars show p50/p90/p99 and are capped at 100 ms.

## Preload and table readiness

All preloads used ordinary APIs with 25-item batches. The latest AuxStorage runs used 16 concurrent batch writers and reported zero HTTP retries. No native bulk import was used.

| Backend                 | Preload seconds | Minutes | Storage measurement                                                   |
| ----------------------- | --------------: | ------: | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| AuxStorage/PostgreSQL   |          967.75 |   16.13 | 82.25 GB data directory including WAL, after splits; no native census |
| AuxStorage/FoundationDB |        1,401.11 |   23.35 | 111.811 GB KV data; 128.006 GB disk used, after splits                |
| AuxStorage/SQLite       |        1,449.68 |   24.16 | 130.73 GB main.db plus 210 MB WAL; 130.94 GB directory, after splits  |
| Dynoxide                |        4,209.65 |   70.16 | 114.98 GB SQLite file at qualification                                |
| ExtendDB                |        7,690.39 |  128.17 | 151.45 GB database at qualification                                   |

A historical Dynoxide setup attempt spent **more than 17 minutes in DescribeTable** on the 25-million-item table before measurement began. The inspected implementation recounts the main table and calculates index sizes through joins. These measured runs bypassed DescribeTable.

## System configuration

| Setting                  | Configuration                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Hosts                    | AWS Spot m8gd.4xlarge, eu-north-1; local NVMe                                                                                      |
| Placement                | one separate same-AZ test runner m8gd.4xlarge runner for all splits                                                                |
| Native database budget   | Four cores / 16 GiB                                                                                                                |
| Application budget       | Two cores / 8 GiB; PostgreSQL applications shared this budget with PgBouncer                                                       |
| AuxStorage/SQLite budget | Embedded database and application shared six cores / 24 GiB, swap disabled                                                         |
| Dynoxide budget          | Embedded SQLite and application shared six cores / 24 GiB                                                                          |
| Versions                 | PostgreSQL 18.6; FoundationDB 7.4.8; Dynoxide 1.5.0; ExtendDB 0.1.14                                                               |
| FoundationDB             | Four pinned processes, single redundancy, ssd-redwood-1; each --memory 8GiB --cache-memory 2GiB, combined four-core / 16 GiB slice |
| Transport                | HTTP for AuxStorage and Dynoxide; HTTPS for ExtendDB                                                                               |
| Index consistency        | Immediate GSI maintenance; ExtendDB index_propagation_delay_ms=0                                                                   |
| Streams / TTL            | Disabled; no TTL values supplied                                                                                                   |

AuxStorage/PostgreSQL used fixed admission: 16 foreground slots and a 25 ms queue wait. FoundationDB and SQLite used adaptive admission with initial limit 100. FoundationDB run b recovered to 100 within 10 seconds after each overloaded split; SQLite remained stable at 100 with no admission rejections. AuxStorage database slices used CPUs 0–3 / 16 GiB and application slices CPUs 4–5 / 8 GiB; embedded SQLite used CPUs 0–5 / 24 GiB. Swap was disabled.

The fixture used 10,000 partition keys, 2,500 items per partition, and every item populated all three ALL GSIs. Reads mixed GetItem, ten-item BatchGetItem and Query equally; queries sampled the main table and all three GSIs, returning 20–100 full items. Writes mixed PutItem, UpdateItem, ten-item transactions and 25-item batches equally. Every PutItem was conditioned and one quarter intentionally failed: 25% of write requests were conditioned, with 6.25% intentional condition failures over complete schedule cycles.

Each split rested 120 seconds after readiness, then added 16 req/s every five seconds. AuxStorage and Dynoxide started at 50 req/s; ExtendDB started at 5 req/s. Latency includes runner scheduling, network and response validation. Operations sampled the full fixed keyspace; payloads varied by item and revision. Seeds were 1, 1000000007 and 2000000011 for 90/10, 50/50 and 10/90 respectively.

## PostgreSQL configuration

Both AuxStorage/PostgreSQL and ExtendDB used these PostgreSQL settings:

| Setting                                       | Value    |
| --------------------------------------------- | -------- |
| shared_buffers                                | 4 GiB    |
| effective_cache_size                          | 9830 MiB |
| work_mem                                      | 8 MB     |
| maintenance_work_mem                          | 512 MB   |
| max_wal_size                                  | 8 GB     |
| checkpoint_timeout                            | 15 min   |
| checkpoint_completion_target                  | 0.9      |
| random_page_cost                              | 1.25     |
| effective_io_concurrency                      | 64       |
| autovacuum_max_workers                        | 4        |
| Vacuum / analyze scale factors                | 0.02     |
| wal_compression                               | lz4      |
| default_statistics_target                     | 100      |
| pg_stat_statements                            | Enabled  |
| fsync / full_page_writes / synchronous_commit | Enabled  |

PgBouncer used transaction pooling with prepared-statement support. AuxStorage used 20 ordinary server connections plus four reserve connections and a 20-connection application pool. ExtendDB used 24 total native server connections, with both catalog and data connections through PgBouncer.
